Grantweft: bounded recovery milestone

Formal mTLS service, installed package, client/gateway/backend namespaces. Positive EOF/reset is required; timeout is not termination. Controlled Linux VM observations, not native/physical performance or an SLO. Qualification scope includes the installed systemd unit: start/stop, crash restart, restart limiting and manual DEGRADED repair; consult the pass flag and per-case outcomes. Additional closeout checks cover enabled systemd boot startup with a read-only pre-service journal witness, a persistent readonly ext4 boot fault and explicit repair, plus a fixed 40-cycle/ten-minute same-process observation. No sudden power loss, actual suspend, boot-zero-gap or long-term qualification. Approval/revocation revision conflicts conservatively degrade the service. Actual tmpfs exhaustion, live directory write denial, readonly-filesystem startup and SQLite page-budget failures are exercised. Recovery preserves the catalog and requires repair plus restart; no online reset or automatic GC. Production readiness is not claimed.
